# Source-discovery selection decisions

This is the normalized content in its original document order with a source-discovery semantic overlay. The labels are annotations; the content beneath them remains the normalized source structure.

- **Green — SELECTED:** relevant, current material eligible for extraction.
- **Orange — SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY:** possibly relevant, time-bounded, or temporally unknown material that remains eligible for extraction.
- **Gray — HISTORICAL:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **Blue — FUTURE:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **White — NOT SELECTED / UNASSESSED:** unchanged content without an accepted current extraction decision.

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b464</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">What documents are needed to apply for an installment purchase?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b465</code><br><small>documents · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Specifies documentation required from applicants to obtain installment financing in-store.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">To make a purchase on installment, take your identification document and social card or identification card with you when going to the store.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b466</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">What payment methods can I use to repay the loan for a purchase made on installment?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b467</code><br><small>repayment · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Details the channels and procedures available for loan repayment on installment purchases.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">After making a purchase on installment through Ameriabank, you can make regular repayments and other relevant payments using the most convenient methods for you: via payment terminals, money transfer from other banks, at our bank branches, and of course through Ameriabank’s online system on the Bank’s website.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b468</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Who can use the offered installment option?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b469</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>States core customer eligibility criteria including residency, age, and citizenship requirements for installment financing.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Installment financing is available for resident individuals aged 20 to 66, both with and without citizenship of the Republic of Armenia.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b470</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">What products can I purchase through installment?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b471</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Defines the product categories eligible to be financed under the installment loan.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Products eligible for installment financing include household and computer equipment, smartphones, solar systems, furniture, and more.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b472</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">How is the payable amount transferred in case of an installment purchase?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b473</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Specifies the disbursement mechanism and fund transfer terms for approved installment financing.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The approved installment financing amount is transferred to the store in non-cash form based on your instruction as stipulated in the contract.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b474</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">How is the financing decision made?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b475</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Outlines underwriting decision criteria, credit limit determination, and explicit grounds for rejection.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The decision on installment financing, the maximum loan amount, as well as the grounds for rejection, are determined through assessment of the customer&#x27;s creditworthiness.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b476</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Outlines underwriting decision criteria, credit limit determination, and explicit grounds for rejection.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Grounds for rejecting installment financing may include:</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b477</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Outlines underwriting decision criteria, credit limit determination, and explicit grounds for rejection.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">a) poor credit history,</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #12b76a;background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED</strong> &nbsp; <code>b478</code><br><small>eligibility · relevant · current · llm</small><br><small>Outlines underwriting decision criteria, credit limit determination, and explicit grounds for rejection.</small></div>

<span style="background:#ecfdf3;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">b) unreliable information provided.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b479</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Can I cancel the installment?</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b480</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · unknown · llm</small><br><small>Contains statutory and contractual terms regarding credit contract termination and interest calculation under consumer finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Yes, within 7 (seven) working days (unless a longer period is specified in the contract), you can cancel the provided financing without any justification by unilaterally terminating the contract.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b481</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · unknown · llm</small><br><small>Contains statutory and contractual terms regarding credit contract termination and interest calculation under consumer finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">In that case, the buyer is obliged to pay the lender interest for the use of the credit amount, which is calculated based on the annual effective interest rate specified in the credit agreement.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b482</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · unknown · llm</small><br><small>Contains statutory and contractual terms regarding credit contract termination and interest calculation under consumer finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">No other compensation is provided for termination of the financing contract.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>t1</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · unknown · llm</small><br><small>Official comprehensive terms matrix for goods-purchase consumer finance including limits, tenor, fees, penalties, and eligibility criteria.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">CREDIT HISTORY AND CREDIT SCORE SUMMARY (SCORE) INFORMATION</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b490</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">This document presents the information required by RA legislation regarding credit history and credit score summary (score), the factors affecting it, and their use in the lending process by Ameriabank CJSC (hereinafter referred to as the &quot;Bank&quot;).</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b491</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · llm</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Credit history is the information created/processed by a credit bureau regarding a customer&#x27;s financial obligations, reflecting the customer&#x27;s debts, payments, payment habits, and the dynamics/history of this information.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b492</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Credit history includes, in particular, the identification data of the customer, the amount of each obligation, annual interest rate, outstanding balance, payment frequency and due dates, delays in payments, guarantees provided for third parties, depersonalized information about obligations of related parties, as well as data on inquiries made about the credit history.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b493</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">As a rule, credit history is used by financial institutions to assess a customer&#x27;s ability to assume/maintain financial obligations, act as a guarantor, evaluate lending opportunities either at the initiative of the customer or the Bank, or provide loan offers to the customer.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b494</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Based on the customer&#x27;s consent, the Bank submits an inquiry to ACRA Credit Reporting. Credit history information is reflected in a credit report that contains the customer&#x27;s credit history for the previous 5 years as of the date of inquiry.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b495</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The customer can review their credit history at http://www.acra.am/.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b496</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The credit score is a numerical evaluation of the customer&#x27;s creditworthiness and reliability.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b497</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The Bank uses its own creditworthiness and reliability evaluation system for lending purposes, with the main influencing factors listed below:</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b498</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Credit history: The number and amount of active loans, frequency of loan applications, and a positive credit history (no delays, high-quality servicing) can help improve the score and lead to loan approval. A poor credit history (delays, long-term delinquencies) may lower the score and result in rejection or stricter conditions.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b499</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Employment, income, and work experience: Having stable employment/income can increase the score and lead to approval. Unstable or irregular income/employment may lower the score and lead to rejection or stricter terms.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b500</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Credit burden: A high debt-to-income ratio can negatively impact the score, while a low credit burden can help improve the score and lead to approval.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b501</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">FICO Score: A FICO score is a statistical scoring model based on credit history used globally to assess credit risk. Details at acra.am.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b502</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Other information characterizing the customer</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b503</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">The importance of credit history and score</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b504</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Credit history and credit score play a critical role in the decision-making process for loan approval, allowing banks and credit organizations to assess the level of credit risk and predict the customer’s likelihood of proper repayment.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b505</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Incorrect or incomplete credit history</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b506</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">If the credit history contains errors or incomplete data, the customer may apply to ACRA Credit Reporting CJSC (credit bureau) or the financial organization that provided the incorrect data.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b507</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">For inquiries to ACRA Credit Reporting CJSC, the procedure and detailed description are available at acra.am.</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b508</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Steps to improve credit history and credit score</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b515</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

<span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">To improve credit history and score, it is necessary to eliminate the root causes as soon as possible, particularly:</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b516</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Make regular loan payments according to the schedule, avoiding even a single day’s delay</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b517</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Reduce the number and amount of guarantees provided; if possible, ensure repayment of overdue guaranteed obligations</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b518</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Ease the credit burden by paying off overdue obligations</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b519</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>

- <span style="background:#fff4e5;padding:0.08em 0.18em;">Avoid frequent applications for new loans, as credit history inquiries (except those for loan monitoring) may negatively impact the score</span>

<div style="border-left:5px solid #f79009;background:#fff4e5;padding:0.55em 0.8em;margin:1.2em 0 0.65em 0;"><strong>SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY</strong> &nbsp; <code>b520</code><br><small>product_terms · relevant · time_bounded · inherited</small><br><small>Contains official terms references with explicit validity periods, statutory credit history disclosure, and credit scoring information for consumer installment finance.</small></div>
